Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any protozoa module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 283. The projective resolution has length at most 998 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_283(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 283. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^998(S, F) is trivial. QED.
